國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國消費回暖、房市降溫，Fed 政策轉向與川習會成市場焦點 
經濟 
⚫ 8 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額 7,739 億美元，月增 1.2%、年增 6.0%，
扭轉前月下滑走勢；核心零售(扣除汽車、零件及加油站)月增 1.2%。其
中非實體零售商、加油站及電子家電商店銷售成長，顯示美國消費需求
仍具支撐力道。 
⚫ 8 月美國住宅市場表現分化，建築許可年化總數降至 139.4 萬戶，月減
2.7%；新屋開工降至 127.5 萬戶，月減2.6%，惟單戶住宅開工升至 91.8
萬戶 、 創近5 個月新高 。 另一方面 ， 在建住宅年減3.2%、 完工年減27.1%，
顯示高利率與需求不確定性仍抑制整體住宅建設活動。 
⚫ 9 月聯邦公開市場委員會(FOMC)決議以 12 比 0 一致升息 1 碼至 3.75%
至 4.00%，為 2023 年 7 月以來首度升息。委員會認為美國經濟及勞動
市場仍具韌性，惟通膨持續高於 2%目標，因此採取升息行動並重申致
力維持價格穩定，政策立場轉趨鷹派；同時刪除前瞻指引措辭，強調未
來政策將依據經濟數據及展望調整。最新經濟預測(SEP)顯示，2026 年
GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%、失業率下修至 4.1%，PCE 及核心 PCE 通膨
率則分別上修至 3.7%及 3.4%，利率中位數升至 4.1%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 在 9 月 24 日美國華府「川習會」前，美國財長貝森特與中國副總理何
立峰於紐約會談，聚焦AI、關稅、貿易休戰及關鍵礦產供應等議題。市
場預期雙方將以維持對話、避免貿易緊張升級為主，短期內難有重大突
破。 
⚫ 美國在 USMCA 談判中要求墨西哥提高 AI 伺服器及半導體產品的北美
原產地比重，並限制中國等非北美零組件使用，以防堵企業藉由墨西哥
組裝規避美國關稅。由於墨西哥已成為美國 AI 伺服器重要供應基地，
若新規定上路 ， 恐促使包括台廠在內的供應鏈提高北美在地採購與生產
比重，進一步重塑北美 AI 硬體供應鏈布局。 
⚫ 美國總統川普簽署《2026 年 Lindsey O. Graham 制裁俄羅斯與伊朗法
案》 ，強化對俄、伊制裁，並授權對大量採購俄羅斯能源國家商品課徵
最高 100%關稅。

短評 
⚫ 美國 8 月零售銷售顯著回升，顯示消費支出與內需動能仍具韌性，持續
支撐美國經濟擴張；惟住宅市場呈現單戶住宅相對穩健、多戶住宅及整
體營建活動偏弱的分化格局 ， 反映高利率環境仍對房市形成壓力 。 另Fed
升息 1 碼並上修通膨及利率預測，釋出更偏鷹派訊號。此外，9 月 24 日
川習會前美中積極磋商關稅調降方案 ， 若能延續貿易休戰並達成部分降
稅共識，將有助降低全球經貿不確定性並穩定市場信心。

短評 
⚫ AI 相關投資需求持續帶動出口及企業資本支出成長，顯示整體經濟動
能仍具韌性。同時，通膨雖受政府補貼影響而有所抑制，但服務價格與
成本轉嫁趨勢延續，預期通膨仍將緩步升溫。在景氣與物價表現符合預
期下，央行持續推動貨幣政策正常化，不排除於 12 月再度升息。

國

商先於 9 月 20 日舉行，涉及約 300 億美元商品的降稅議題進行討論，
並力求延長將於 11 月到期的貿易休戰協議。

衝擊，但也面臨經濟結構轉型與財政紀律的考驗。

⚫ 台灣央行於 9 月 17 日召開理監事會議，決議維持政策利率 （重貼現率）
於 2%不變，並適度鬆綁選擇性信用管制措施，包括將自然人第二戶購
屋貸款最高成數由 6 成提高至 7 成 ， 以及刪除購地貸款須於一定期間內
動工興建之規定。 
短評 ⚫ 本次理監事會議的政策方向呈現利率維持審慎偏緊基調 、 房市管制微幅鬆綁。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.9.21 
一、 美國—消費回暖、房市降溫，Fed 政策轉向與川習會成市場焦點 
2026 年 8 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額經季節性調整後為 7,739 億美元，較
上月增加 1.2%，優於 7 月修正後月減 0.5%的表現，年增率亦達 6.0%，顯示美國
消費支出動能於 8 月明顯回升。若扣除汽車及零件銷售，零售額月增1.4%、年增
6.9%；進一步扣除汽車、零件及加油站後，核心零售銷售月增1.2%、年增 5.6%，
反映核心消費需求維持穩健成長。從主要業別表現觀察，非實體零售商銷售額月
增 2.6%、加油站月增 3.1%、電子及家電商店月增 1.6%，食品服務及飲酒場所亦
月增 1.2%，顯示線上消費、能源相關支出及服務性消費持續成長；另一方面，建
築材料與園藝用品經銷商月減 0.2%，對整體零售表現形成部分抵銷。整體而言，
8 月美國零售銷售由前月下滑轉為明顯成長，且扣除汽車及能源相關項目後仍維
持穩健增幅，顯示美國消費需求仍具支撐力道，對經濟活動形成正面挹注，惟後
續仍須持續關注物價變動、金融情勢及消費信心對零售支出的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國住宅市場持續呈現分化走勢，建築許可年化總數降至 139.4
萬戶，月減 2.7%，惟較去年同期增加 3.5%；其中單戶住宅建築許可 87.8 萬戶，
月減 1.8%，5 戶以上多戶住宅建築許可 46.7 萬戶，月減 3.1%，顯示住宅開發規
劃動能較前月略有降溫 。 另一方面 ， 新屋開工年化數降至127.5 萬戶 ， 月減2.6%、
年減 1.2%，其中單戶住宅開工回升至 91.8 萬戶，月增 7.6%、年增 5.2%，為近 5
個月最高水準 ； 惟5 戶以上多戶住宅開工降至 34.4 萬戶 ， 月減22.5%、 年減15.5%，
拖累整體開工表現。施工方面，在建住宅年化數為 127.1 萬戶，月增 0.3%，但較
去年同期減少 3.2%，顯示住宅建設規模仍呈收斂趨勢；完工年化數則降至 112.8
萬戶，月減 11.9%、年減 27.1%，反映住宅供給落成速度明顯放緩。整體而言，8
月美國住宅市場呈現單戶住宅需求相對具支撐、但多戶住宅及整體營建活動偏弱
的格局，顯示在高利率與需求前景仍具不確定性的環境下，建商投資態度仍趨審
慎，後續仍須關注利率走勢及房市需求變化對住宅市場的影響。 
2026 年 9 月聯邦公開市場委員會(FOMC)會議決議以 12 比 0 一致同意將聯邦
基金利率目標區間調升 1 碼至 3.75%至 4.00%，為 2023 年 7 月以來首度升息，謹
將本次會議聲明 、 最新經濟預測摘要(SEP)及主席 Kevin Warsh 記者會重點摘錄如

下：(一)儘管地緣政治風險推升經濟前景不確定性，美國經濟活動仍維持穩健擴
張，國內支出展現韌性，生產力成長與資本投資維持強勁，勞動市場表現穩定，
就業增幅大致與勞動力成長相符，失業率變動不大；(二)通膨仍高於聯準會 2%目
標，委員會認為近期數據尚未充分顯示通膨正以足夠速度回落至目標水準，因此
決議升息 1 碼，以支持通膨更及時回歸 2%目標，並重申將致力實現價格穩定；
(三)本次會議在政策溝通上出現調整，聲明刪除過去偏向寬鬆的前瞻指引措辭並
簡化內容；主席 Kevin Warsh 於記者會中明確表示不提供前瞻指引，亦不預判未
來會議結果 ， 強調後續政策行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及整體經濟展望決定 ；(四)
最新 SEP 顯示，2026 年實質 GDP 成長率預測中位數由 2.2%上修至 2.3%，失業
率由 4.3%下修至 4.1%；PCE 及核心 PCE 通膨率則分別上修至 3.7%及 3.4%，顯
示通膨壓力仍具黏性 。 利率方面 ，點陣圖顯示 2026 年底聯邦基金利率預測由 3.8%
升至 4.1%，2027 年底維持 4.1%，官員仍保留進一步升息可能，整體政策立場較
6 月轉趨鷹派；(五)主席 Kevin Warsh 表示，美國經濟與勞動市場維持韌性，通膨
仍高於目標且地緣政治風險升溫 ， 為本次升息主因 。 未來聯準會不提供前瞻指引 ，
也不預設利率路徑，將依經濟數據與展望機動調整政策。 
在 9 月 24 日美國華府白宮舉行之 「川習會」 前 ， 美國財長貝森特(Scott Bessent)
與中國國務院副總理何立峰於紐約摩根大通總部舉行會談，美國貿易代表葛里爾
(Jamieson Greer) 亦共同參與。雙方將就人工智慧 (AI)、關稅、貿易休戰及關鍵礦
產供應等議題交換意見，並為川普與習近平會晤預作準備。討論重點包括將於11
月 10 日到期之美中貿易休戰安排、中國稀土磁材及關鍵礦產出口供應情況，以
及 AI 模型安全與治理機制等議題。此外，雙方亦將檢視今年 5 月北京會談後尚
待落實之事項，包括降低部分非戰略性商品關稅、中國增加美國農產品採購及波
音客機採購計畫等。市場普遍預期本次會談將以維持既有合作架構及避免貿易緊
張升級為主要目標，短期內出現重大突破的可能性仍相對有限，美中關稅、關鍵
礦產供應及 AI 治理議題預料將成為本次川習會的重要焦點。 
美國政府近期在美墨加協定(USMCA)檢討談判中，要求墨西哥針對人工智慧
(AI)伺服器、半導體及相關硬體產品建立更嚴格的原產地規範，擬限制來自中國
等非北美地區零組件占比，並可能參照汽車產業的區域含量標準，以防堵外國企
業透過墨西哥組裝後出口美國，規避美國對中國商品實施的關稅與貿易限制。市
場分析指出，在美國 AI 基礎建設需求帶動下，墨西哥已成為重要 AI 伺服器生產

用相關內容。

6

基地，今年上半年 AI 資料中心伺服器出口金額達 830 億美元，其中 94%銷往美
國，並超越汽車成為對美最大出口項目。由於鴻海等台灣電子代工廠在墨西哥設
有 AI 伺服器生產據點，若相關規範正式上路，供應鏈可能被迫提高北美在地採
購及生產比重，進一步重塑北美 AI 硬體供應鏈布局。美墨雙方預計 9 月底於華
府展開新一輪談判，惟相關原產地門檻、過渡期間及適用範圍尚未定案。 
美國總統川普已於 9 月 18 日簽署《2026 年 Lindsey O. Graham 制裁俄羅斯與
伊朗法案》，該法案此前分別以參議院 86 票對 11 票、眾議院 262 票對 159 票通
過。法案除擴大對俄羅斯政府官員、金融機構及協助能源運輸的「影子艦隊」制
裁外，亦授權總統對持續大量購買俄羅斯石油及天然氣的第三國商品課徵最高
100%關稅，其中中國、印度等俄羅斯能源主要買家可能受到影響。法案目的在於
削弱俄羅斯能源出口收入及其支撐烏俄戰爭的財政來源，並強化對伊朗的相關制
裁。 
二、歐元區—美歐矛盾與歐中貿易協商在即，增添工業復甦變數 
 歐元區 7 月工業生產較 6 月下滑 0.1%，為連續兩個月衰退，其中非耐久消費
財產出減少 1.6%，抵銷中間財、資本財、消費耐久財分別成長0.3%、0.5%、0.9%
的影響，主要是美伊衝突帶來的庫存儲備需求持續消退，抵銷德國軍備採購及消
暑用品消費增加的影響。以國別而言，德國工業生產下跌1.5%最為疲軟，且為該
國近一年最大跌幅，主要是部分汽車大廠決定提前進行歲修，故預期待歲修結束
後，8、9 月份可望出現汽車庫存回補的生產需求，此外，在德國軍備採購需求的
支持下 ， 德國7 月工業訂單仍大幅成長 2.5%， 相關產出可望在第三季末及第四季
陸續反映；法、義、西工業生產月增率則分別為-0.4%、0.7%、0.6%。 
 整體而言，美伊衝突爆發後，能源密集產業普遍增產，但該類產業從 6 月起
已有較明顯的趨緩跡象，其他主要產業則大致呈現橫向波動，顯示工業生產趨勢
仍屬溫和。惟近期歐盟提議讓加拿大成為歐盟準會員國，引發美國不滿，可能進
一步升高美歐貿易與外交摩擦；此外，歐盟與中國預定於 10 月 8 日針對貿易失
衡問題進行重要協商 ， 後續若涉及貿易措施調整 ， 亦可能影響歐元區製造業表現 。

7 月核心機械訂單月減 3.7%，其中製造業訂單小幅下滑，主要受到化學
產業訂單減少影響，惟電子產品及電氣機械訂單持續成長，反映人工智慧相

關產業及供應鏈企業仍積極擴充產能，支撐資本支出動能。 雖然非製造業訂
單則因資通訊及資訊服務業需求回落影響而表現較弱，但在人力短缺與數位
轉型需求支撐下，投資動能仍具韌性。綜合來看，企業獲利穩健及AI 投資熱
潮延續，預期日本企業資本支出仍將維持成長態勢。 
8 月出口年增 19.3%，半導體相關產品仍為主要成長動能，其中半導體等
電子零件出口大幅成長 52.3%。從地區來看，對美國、中國及歐盟出口分別
成長 24.9%、20.6%及 11.0%，半導體等電子零組件對中國出口金額，以及對
美國 、 歐盟的半導體製造設備出口均較去年同期倍增，顯示全球 AI 及高科技
產業投資需求持續支撐日本出口表現。 
8 月全國消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 1.9%，主要受到政府恢復電力與天然
氣補貼，以及米價回落抑制食品價格所致。從細項來看，石油產品價格上漲
帶動家庭消費品等核心商品價格續漲；服務類通膨除通訊費用趨穩外，其他
服務項目已開始出現漲價跡象。展望未來，能源成本上升及企業將 薪資成本
逐步轉嫁至售價，預料將推動通膨壓力由商品擴散至更多服務領域，使通膨
維持緩步升溫趨勢，並進一步支持央行持續推動貨幣政策正常化。 
日本央行於 9 月 17、18 日召開貨幣政策會議，宣布將政策利率調升 1 碼
至 1.25%。雖然政策聲明整體基調變化不大，但仍持續強調通膨上行風險，
並指出目前金融條件仍相對寬鬆。 值得關注的是 ， 本次決策出現兩張反對票 ，
顯示委員會內部對經濟與通膨前景看法分歧加劇，一派認為當前經濟與通膨
動能尚不足以支持升息，另一派則認為通膨升溫風險高於預期。在會後記者
會上，總裁植田和男指出，日本貨幣政策已進入新的階段，由過去較重視通
膨低於目標的風險，轉為兼顧通膨上、下行風險，並強調未來升息步調將視
經濟與物價發展而定，不會預先承諾特定路徑。此外，他也表示 主要國家央
行升息對日本匯率及金融環境具有重要影響，因此將持續納入政策考量。雖
然總裁未透露最終利率水準，但相關談話暗示目前 1.25%的政策利率可能尚
未達到中性水準，日本央行亦未排除未來進一步升息的可能性。整體而言，
日本央行對通膨前景看法趨於樂觀，政策立場仍偏向緊縮，預期若未來數季
通膨持續走揚，日本央行仍有望於 12 月再度升息。

四、中國—8 月內需持續疲乏，與歐美的關係將為近期關注重點 
在內需方面，1-8 月累計零售額年增率較 2025 年同期增加 1.1%，較 1-7 月累
計的 1.2%持續下降。其中 8 月零售額年增率放緩 0.2 個百分點至 0.4%，反映以
舊換新的補貼力道不如過去兩年水準，加上房市低迷，以及對景氣波動較為敏感
的消費耐久財需求進一步下滑 ，8 月家具 、 汽車 、 裝潢消費分別年減7.9%、18.5%、
11.8%。另外，1-8 月固定資產投資年減幅由 6.7%擴大至 7.2%，反映美伊衝突、
政府主導去化過剩產能、美國貿易戰等因素導致民間投資需求急遽萎縮；萎縮產
業包含化工、醫藥、專用設備、汽車製造業、金屬製品業等。同期間房地產開發
投資額年減幅由 19.2%擴大至 19.9%、施工面積年減幅則擴大為 13.2%，新建商
品房銷售面積年減幅擴大為 13%，房市投資、買氣皆持續低迷。 
受到出口維持高度成長帶動 ， 抵銷內需疲軟的影響 ，8 月工業生產年增率較 7
月回升 0.7 個百分點至 5.2%(1-8 月工業生產年增率持平 1-7 月水準為 5.3%)。依
產業別來看，8 月份高科技產品仍是主要成長動能，包含 IC(成長 20.6%)、工業
機器人(34.6%)、服務機器人(5%)、 新能源車(21.9%)等 ； 惟受到原油供應減少以及
能源價格上漲壓力影響，化纖、水泥、玻璃、鋼材等產量持續衰退，另因 3C 關
鍵物料漲價影響，手機產量衰退幅度高達 22.3%，太陽能電池則因取消出口退稅
政策跌幅高達 12.9%。展望未來，若9 月 24 日川習會有正向發展，美中關稅休戰
得以延長 ， 且歐中貿易摩擦未進一步惡化 ， 將有助於穩定中國出口與製造業景氣 ，
抵銷內需疲軟的經濟壓力。

飲料（5.95%）、交通運輸（4.6%）餐飲和住宿服務（8.38%）及個人照護/社
會安全 （15.17%） 價格亦大幅上漲。儘管通膨水準仍維持在印度央行目標區間
內，惟生產成本居高不下，恐進一步外溢到消費端，並衝擊內需表現。

五、台灣—央行維持利率不變並適度鬆綁不動產信用管制措施 
台灣央行於 9 月 17 日召開理監事會議，決議維持政策利率(重貼現率)於 2%
不變，央行指出，雖然國際油價維持高檔、服務類通膨仍具黏性，但國內通膨仍
屬可控範圍，2026 年 1 至 8 月通膨年增 1.84%，預估 2027 年可回落至 2%以下；
同時考量中東地緣政治、美國經貿政策及 AI 產業發展等不確定因素，因此採取
審慎觀望態度，暫不升息。 
此外，央行認為先前實施之選擇性信用管制已逐漸見效，房市交易降溫、不
動產貸款集中度下降，因此適度調整相關措施，包括將自然人第二戶購屋貸款最
高成數由 6 成提高至 7 成，並刪除購地貸款須切結於一定期間內動工興建之規
定，改由金融機構依授信實務審慎評估辦理。整體而言，本次政策方向呈現利率
維持審慎偏緊基調、房市管制微幅鬆綁。

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Hon Hai (2317.TW): Sep beat: revenues +38% YoY; New AI servers, CPO 
switch and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26; Buy (on CL)
5 October 2026 | 11:23PM HKT
Hon Hai’s Sep revenues were +38% YoY/ 26% MoM to NT$1.2tn, or 15% / 21% 
higher than our / Bloomberg consensus estimates, reflecting strong AI servers ramp 
up and new smartphone models in contribution. From a MoM perspective, 
Consumer electronics is the strongest, followed by Components, Cloud and 
Networking and Computing, and all segments are positive MoM. The strong MoM 
reaffirms our positive view on the company’s leading market position across: (1) 
Smartphone: serving global leading brands, which suffer less under high memory 
costs, with new model launches and form factor changes (report link), driving the 
shipment and ASP increase, (2) AI servers: serving global leading CSP customers, 
and early in the delivery of the latest AI server racks, driving the shipment and ASP 
increase. The company is also the major supplier of CPO switch, supporting Sep and 
4Q26 growth. From a YoY perspective, Cloud and Networking is the strongest, 
followed by Components, Computing, and Consumer electronics, and all segments 
are positive YoY. We attribute the slower YoY in consumer electronics to its major 
brand customers’ fewer model launches this year, along with the new form factor 
(foldable phones) being not available in Sep, and a modest smartphone market 
under high memory costs.

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +37% 
YoY/ +18% QoQ to NT$3.6 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 
47%, or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both 
YoY and MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new 
smartphone models, and CPO switches. Management notes that AI-related products

remain on track to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products

EV business in Sep 2026: Hon Hai’s EV subsidiary Foxtron (2258.TW, not covered) 
reported Sep revenues of NT$1.1bn (vs. Aug-26 / Sep-25 revenues at NT$914mn /

while EV registrations delivered robust sequential momentum, more than doubling MoM 
to 7,026 units (vs. 3,259 units in August). Notably, Foxtron ranked No.2 with cumulative 
YTD sales volume reaching 3,138 units (news).

Earnings revision: We factor in Hon Hai Sep revenues and revise up 2026E net income 
by 2% to reflect the strong beat, mainly on higher revenues. We remain positive on AI 
server racks ramp up, Hon Hai’s leading market position in terms of scale, technology, 
and execution, and rising foldable phones to drive growth ahead. We keep 2027E/ 
2028E estimates largely unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 17.9x (unchanged) is derived from a peers’ 
average ratio of trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the 
sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our target P/E of 17.9x is at the high-end of the company’s 
historical forward P/E, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from 
the competitive consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which 
should drive profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. 
Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design,

their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月贸易数据点评：进出口增速上行，AI、价格为主要拉动.pdf
Report source: 万联证券
Date: 2026-10-14

Status: ok

### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
进出口增速上行，AI、价格为主要拉动

[Table_ReportType] 
——9月贸易数据点评

[Table_ReportDate] 2026年 10月 14日

[T able_Summary] ⚫ 事件：9月，按美元计价，我国出口当月同比36.3%，前值
25%；进口同比31%，前值28.2%；贸易顺差1335亿美元。 
⚫ 出口增速攀升，外需支撑仍强，产业拉动特征持续。海外制造
业景气度依然较高，韩国出口增速持续走高，半导体出口保持
旺盛。美国8 月制造业库存同比增速继续上行，新订单高位小
幅放缓，经济韧性依然较强，当前加息和通胀的压力尚未显
现。关税预期和全球航运成本持续上升影响下， “抢出口”效
应带来一定推动。高新技术产品、机电产品出口保持上行，AI
产业拉动仍强。我国出口集装箱运价持续走高，出口价格指数
继续上行。人民币持续升值，短期对出口影响较小。 
⚫ 抢出口、基数效应拉动下，对美国出口持续修复。我国对东
盟、非洲、俄罗斯出口保持旺盛。对美出口增速上行，关税政
策不确定性担忧，叠加海外四季度圣诞节等假日备货旺季需求
带动， “抢出口”行为明显。9月中美双方“300亿对300亿”
对等降税政策，给我国轻工消费品出口带来一定利好。欧盟对
我国贸易壁垒政策趋严，未来仍面临一定压力。我国对韩国当
月出口增速大幅上行，主要是AI产业驱动影响。 
⚫ AI 产品出口保持旺盛，汽车出口上行，劳动密集型产品出口
持续改善。集成电路、自动数据处理设备出口金额同比持续攀
升，价格上涨的驱动作用仍强。下游汽车增速上行，新能源汽
车出口退税率2027年 4月即将下调， “抢出口”带来一定推
动；船舶制造业出口持续放缓。劳动密集型产品出口跌幅持续
收窄，低基数和关税政策带来一定拉动。原料端钢材、铝材、
成品油出口上行，稀土持续回落。农产品出口表现分化。 
⚫ 进口上行超市场预期，价格拉动作用加强。进口价格总指数再
度走高，大宗商品、半导体、存储设备等价格上涨对进口的支
持作用加强。AI产业链进口保持旺盛，价格影响下，能源、
金属材料进口表现分化，农产品进口增速回落。 
⚫ 价格、AI 产业拉动效果明显，出口全年增速有望维持高位。
我国全年出口为经济的重要拉动分项，前三季度整体延续上行
趋势。海外需求整体表现较好，劳动密集型产品出口温和修
复，AI产业链呈现较强的拉动作用，但仍需关注半导体、数
据处理设备等产品的价格和库存水平。地缘政治影响下，大宗
商品价格持续高位，价格对进出口的支撑作用仍强。11月将
迎来美国中期选举，未来关税政策仍有调整风险。随着“抢出
口”效应逐步释放，年底出口仍面临一定不确定性，但外需的
支撑作用仍强。 
⚫ 风险因素：海外需求回落幅度超预期，中美贸易关系变动超预
期，地缘政治风险发展超预期。

事件 
2026年9月， 按美元计价， 我国出口4471.84亿美元， 当月同比36.3%（市场预期25.7%），
前值25%， 累计同比由19.3%继续上行至21.3%；进口金额3136.52亿美元， 当月同比31%
（市场预期23.8%），前值28.2%，累计同比由27%上行至27.5%；贸易顺差1335.32亿美
元，前值1190.85亿美元，贸易差额当月同比由17.9%上行至50.5%。按人民币计价，
9月出口当月同比29.6%，上月为18.6%，进口同比24.6%，上月为21.7%。

1    出口增速攀升，AI产业链拉动作用延续 
出口增速持续走高，外需支撑仍强，产业拉动特征持续。按美元计价，9月我国出口
4471.84亿美元，当月同比由8月的25%上行至36.3%，显著高于市场预期的25.7%，增
速持续攀升，累计同比由19.3%继续上行至21.3%。美国、日本、欧元区制造业PMI高
位小幅回落，全球制造业PMI上行，海外制造业景气度依然较高。韩国出口增速由68.7%
继续上行至83.5%，半导体出口保持旺盛。美国8月制造业库存同比增速继续上行，新
订单高位小幅放缓，美国经济韧性依然较强，当前加息和通胀的压力尚未显现。关税
预期和全球航运成本持续上升影响下， “抢出口”效应带来一定推动。高新技术产品
出口累计同比由42.9%进一步走高至45.6%，AI产业链出口驱动作用仍强。 机电产品出
口累计同比由26.9%上行至29%，仍是出口主要推动项。

图表1：韩国出口增速高位持续上行（%） 图表2：美国制造业存货持续上行，新增订单回落(%)

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

对东盟、非洲出口保持旺盛，抢出口、基数效应拉动下，对美国出口持续修复，但中
长期贸易关系依然承压。前9个月我国对东盟、非洲、俄罗斯出口增速领跑整体，9月
增速延续高位，出口保持旺盛。对美国出口累计同比上行3.6个百分点至9.8%，当月
同比也继续上行，低基数起到一定抬升作用。美国中期选举将至，关税政策存在一定
不确定性，叠加海外四季度圣诞节等假日备货旺季需求带动， “抢出口”行为明显。
9月中美双方“300亿对300亿”对等降税框架职责文件、降税产品清单等达成共识，
美国降税行业主要为玩具、小家电、婴儿用品、厨卫用品、节日礼物等轻工消费品，
给我国相关行业出口带来一定利好，中国降税则侧重农产品与资源品行业。欧盟对我
国贸易壁垒政策趋严，行业正从新能源领域逐步扩张到化工、风电等传统制造业，虽

然本月出口在企业“抢出口”避险情绪带动下增速上行，但未来仍面临一定压力。我
国对韩国当月出口增速大幅上行，主要是AI产业驱动影响。 
我国出口集装箱运价持续走高，出口价格指数继续上行，进口价格指数反弹。9月我
国出口集装箱运价指数继续攀升至高位，逐步接近2024年7月的高点。红海危机持续
发酵，霍尔木兹海峡航运受阻，船舶航程大幅拉长，叠加燃油成本和附加费增加，推
动运价持续抬升。从已经公布的8月数据来看，我国出口价格总指数上行，矿产品出
口价格反弹上行， 化工相关产品出口价格高位边际回落； 进口价格指数大幅反弹至近
几年高点。

图表3：我国对不同国家出口累计同比（%） 图表4：出口集装箱运价指数高位震荡

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

人民币仍处升值通道，短期对出口影响有限。美联储9月开启加息，美元指数持续走
高。但人民币在9月延续升值趋势，美元兑人民币在10月中旬已经向下突破6.7。我国
经济表现稳健， 产业结构持续优化，AI行业快速发展， 出口保持旺盛，贸易顺差1335.32
亿美元，前值1190.85亿美元，环比持续扩张，企业结售汇意愿较强，央行外汇占款
同比稳步上行。我国对外出口市场多元化布局，产业链、价格优势明显，企业外汇套
保策略可规避部分外汇风险。但四季度为传统外贸结汇旺季，人民币升值的背景下，
出口企业仍面临一定汇兑损失的风险。

证券研究报告|宏观报告

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 5 页 共 8 页  
AI相关产品出口保持旺盛，汽车出口上行，劳动密集型产品出口持续改善，资源品出
口整体增长，农产品出口表现分化。从出口商品来看，前9个月，我国AI产业链出口
保持旺盛，集成电路出口金额累计同比由103.9%继续攀升至108.1%， 自动数据处理设
备由49.4%上行至54.6%，价格上涨的驱动作用仍强。下游制造业出口分化，但仍具韧
性，船舶出口由35.2%回落至29.9%，跌幅较大；汽车和汽车底盘由53.2%上行至54.3%，
汽车零件也有所上行，新能源汽车出口退税率2027年4月即将下调， “抢出口”带来一
定推动。手机、音视频设备出口增速上行。劳动密集型产品出口跌幅持续收窄，基数
走低和中美“300亿对300亿”对等降税政策带来一定拉动，鞋靴、玩具、灯具等跌幅
收窄，服饰、箱包、家具等增速保持上行。原料端出口大多上行，地缘政治扰动下，
原油价格持续上行，成品油出口增速本月上行幅度相对最大，钢材、铝材增速上行，
稀土在政策影响下高位逐步回落。农产品方面，粮食出口增速小幅回落，水产品出口
上行转正；肥料出口止跌反弹，磷肥涨价形成一定推动，但增速依然偏低，主要受出
口管控政策影响。

图表7：出口分项累计同比增速走势（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

2    进口增速超市场预期，价格拉动作用加强 
进口增速上行超市场预期，价格支撑作用加强。2026年9月，我国进口金额3136.52亿
美元，按美元计价，当月同比由28.2%上行至31%，远高于市场预期的23.8%，累计同
比由27%上行至27.5%。按人民币计价，9月进口同比24.6%，上月为21.7%。进口价格
总指数再度走高，商品价格指数同比持续上行，大宗商品、半导体、存储设备等价格
上涨对进口的支持作用加强。 我国对韩国进口增速由72.5%进一步走高至75.4%，AI产
业发展带动效果仍强。 我国对美国进口增速延续修复， 对东盟国家进口高位继续上行。
对巴西、澳大利亚等资源国进口高位边际放缓，价格上涨带来一定影响。我国对欧盟
进口增速持续承压下滑。整体来看，价格、政策为进口主要影响因素。

### Page 6

[Table_Pagehead]

AI产业链进口保持旺盛，价格影响下，能源、金属材料进口表现分化，农产品进口增
速回落。从进口产品来看，前9个月，我国机电产品进口累计同比增速由31.6%上行至
33.3%，高技术产品进口累计同比由40.1%上行至42.2%，与出口同步走强。自动数据
处理设备增速上行18.5个百分点至115%， 上行幅度相对最大， 集成电路进口增速也有
所上行。汽车零件、汽车和汽车底盘进口跌幅进一步加深，飞机进口增速低位下探。
农产品进口整体回落，其中食用植物油进口反弹上行，大豆、鲜、干水果及坚果增速
回落。能源类进口分化，受油价高位波动影响，成品油增速进一步回落，而原油价格
小幅反弹，煤及褐煤增速保持上行，天然气进口持续修复。上游资源品进口也表现分
化，铜矿砂、铁矿砂进口增速回落，钢材、铜材、原木、纸浆进口增速上行。

图表10：煤炭进口上行，成品油进口下跌（%） 图表11：AI产业链进口金额增速持续上行（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

3    总结与展望 
价格、AI产业拉动效果明显，出口全年增速有望维持高位。我国全年出口为经济的重
要拉动分项，前三季度整体延续上行趋势。海外需求整体表现较好，劳动密集型产品
出口温和修复，AI产业链呈现较强的拉动作用，但仍需关注半导体、数据处理设备等

4    风险提示 
海外需求回落幅度超预期，中美贸易关系变动超预期，地缘政治风险发展超预期。

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

EARNINGS PREVIEW/ 현대차 / 2026.10.16

### Page 1

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다. 
Buy (Maintain)

Document 154: AIの雇用への影響をどう読むか

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIの雇用への影響をどう読むか.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-14

Status: ok

### Page 1

米国

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和
証券グループ本社を親会社とする大和証券グループの会社です。内容に関する一切の権利は㈱大和総研にあります。無断での複製・転載・転送等はご遠慮ください。

2026 年 10 月 14 日 全13 頁

AI の雇用への影響をどう読むか 
一部の職種で狭まる若手の「入口」、経験を積む場をどう残すか

AI アナリティックリサーチ室 主任研究員 新田 尭之

[要約] 
 近年の実証研究では、米国を中心に、生成AI の影響を受けやすい一部の職種で若手の
雇用悪化が相次いで報告されている。悪化は主に採用の減少として表れており、最初
の職を得る「入口」が狭まっている。ただし、悪化の一部は ChatGPT 公開前に始まっ
ており、在宅勤務の広がりやコロナ禍の採用増の反動とも重なる。複数の要因が考え
られる中、AI の影響の大きさについて明確な結論は得られていない。 
 若手の「入口」が狭まる一方、米国全体では雇用者数が増えている。もっとも、 雇用
増の多くは民間教育・医療によるもので、幅広い産業には広がっていない。採用も離
職も少ないため、既に職に就いている人は職を失いにくいが、これから職を得る人や
転職を望む人にとっては空席が少ない。さらに、AI による省力化は、人員を減らすよ
り、業務の中身や外注を見直す形で進む例が多い。雇用者数だけでは、こうした仕事
の機会の変化は見えにくい。 
 今後の雇用を分けるのは、AI による値下げや品質向上で需要が広がったり、新しい業
務が生まれたりして、どれだけ人手が必要になるかである。ただし、新たな仕事が生
まれても、若手に回るとは限らない。41 カ国の多国籍企業の分析では、生成AI を導入
した企業の拠点で経験者の雇用が増え、若手の比率が下がった。経験者の多くは、入
口の仕事を通じて育つ。若手の採用を絞る判断は、個々の企業には合理的でも、続け
ば将来の経験者が育たない「育成の空洞化」を招きかねない。若手が経験を積む場を
どう残すかが問われており、社内で人を育てる日本企業も無縁ではいられないだろう。

### Page 2

1

2 / 13

1. はじめに 
近年、生成 AI の進化や普及を背景に、AI が雇用に与える影響について、米国を中心に実証
研究が蓄積されている（図表1）。 
図表 1 生成 AI が雇用に与える影響に関する近年の主な実証研究

（注）各研究の対象・期間・比較方法は異なる。 【 】内は対象の国・地域。「曝露」は仕事の内容が AI で代
替・支援されうる度合い、 「曝露職」はその影響を受けやすい職種を指す。 「高曝露職」は曝露の度合いが高い
職種。 
（出所）各研究より大和総研作成

Chandar and Klein Teeselink [2026]
OECD [2025]
・【米国】テック職の求人で、5年以上の経験を求める割合が上昇
　経験者の雇用が6.7％増え、研究者らはAIが経験者への需要を広げたと解釈
製品の革新や新しい業務が、人手の需要を生む例もある
Humlum and Vestergaard [2026]
・【米国】AI投資を増やした企業ほど、主に製品の革新を通じて売上と雇用が伸びた
・【デンマーク】所得・記録された労働時間への平均的な影響は統計的に有意でなく、
　時間を節約できた利用者の85％は他の業務に充てると回答

### Page 3

1

3 / 13

例えば、スタンフォード大学の研究者らは、給与計算・人事サービスを手掛ける ADP の給与
記録を用いて米国の労働市場を分析し、主に次の 6 点を報告している 1。①AI による経済全体
にわたる広範な雇用喪失は確認されない。②一方、AI の影響を受けやすい職種では、受けにく
い職種に比べて22～25 歳の雇用の伸びが弱く、年長層には同程度の差はみられない。③この若
年層の職種間格差は2026 年半ばにかけて拡大を続けた。④その差は主に離職の増加ではなく、
採用の減少による。⑤雇用の減少はAI が人の作業を代替する使い方の多い職種に集中し、仕事
を補完する使い方では雇用の横ばい・増加との関連がみられる。 ⑥調整は基本給よりも雇用の
面に表れている。 
こうした結果は、AI が雇用に与える影響を、全体の人数だけでなく、年齢や採用・離職、AI
の使われ方などに分けて捉える必要性を示している。そこで、本稿ではこうした実証研究や統
計データを基に、①雇用の悪化が目立つ層と、そのうちAI の影響と読める範囲（第 2 章） 、②
雇用者数だけでは捉えにくい仕事の機会の変化（第3 章 ）、 ③今後の雇用を分ける要因（第4 章）
を検討する。

2. 悪化は「入口」で目立つが、AI だけでは説明できない 
（1）AI の影響を受けやすい職種の若年層を中心に悪化 
近年の実証研究を見ると、米国の若年層で雇用の悪化が目立つ。前述のスタンフォード大学
の研究では、22～25 歳の雇用者数は2026 年6 月にChatGPT 公開時の2022 年11 月を約2％下回
った一方、26 歳以上の各年齢層では増加し、特に35～40 歳では約11％多い（図表2）。 
図表 2 年齢層別に見た雇用者数の推移（米国、2022 年 11 月＝1）

働者（毎月 350 万～500 万人） 。年齢層別人口の変化は未調整。

ただし、若年層の雇用が職種を問わず悪化したわけではない。同研究の職種別の推計では、
AI への曝露（仕事の内容がAI で代替・支援されうる度合い）が最も高い職種群の22～25 歳の
雇用の伸びは、2022 年11 月から2026 年6 月までに、最も低い職種群の雇用の伸びを約18％ポ
イント下回った。若年層の中でも、AI の影響を受けやすい職種で悪化が目立つ。 
さらに、米国国勢調査局の研究者らは、大学の卒業記録と雇用の行政記録を 接続し、専攻別
に卒業生の就職状況を比べた。これによると、 AI への曝露が最も高い専攻の卒業生は、
ChatGPT 公開直後から、卒業翌四半期に就業している割合が曝露の低い専攻の卒業生と比べて
低下し始め、2024 年以降は約5％ポイント低い2。 
米国の約6,500 万人分の職歴データを企業単位で分析した研究でも、生成AI の導入企業では
若手（職位の低い層）の雇用が2023 年初めから6 四半期で比較対象の企業より約9％減った。

卒でない若年層（22～27 歳）の失業率が 2025 年秋を頂点に下がり始めた後も、若年大卒者の

なお、オンラインのフリーランス市場でも、AI の影響を受ける分野では、補完的とみなされ
る技能の分野を含め、経験の浅い人向けの案件が、影響を受けない分野に比べて相対的に減っ

（2）ChatGPT 公開前からの悪化や在宅勤務の広がりなども重なる

第二に、在宅勤務の広がりである。ニューヨーク連銀のブログに掲載された分析によると、
米国の在宅勤務が可能な職業では、若手と年長の大卒者の失業率の差が2019 年から 2024 年に
約 1.4％ポイント広がった 8。これは、在宅勤務が難しい職業の同期間の拡大幅（約0.5％ポイ
ント）を大きく上回る。同分析は、在宅勤務では若手への OJT や上司からの助言が難しくなる
ため、企業が経験の浅い人材の採用をためらいやすくなると指摘している。 米英加豪の採用記
録約 2.4 億件の分析でも、在宅勤務のしやすさを同時に考慮すると、AI 曝露と採用者に占める
若手（職位の低い層）の比率との関係は大きく弱まり、多くの場合、統計的に有意 な関係は確
認できなくなった 9。 
米国外の研究も、若手の雇用の減少を AI だけでは説明していない。デンマークでは、AI 曝
露の高いいくつかの職で若手の雇用が減ったものの、研究者らはその減少はAI チャットボット
を導入した企業によるものではないとしている 10。さらに、韓国銀行の研究者の分析によれば、
韓国では 2022 年 6 月から 2026 年 6 月に 15～29 歳の雇用が 28.5 万人減り、その 94％は AI の
影響を受けやすい産業で生じた 11。同研究は、コロナ禍の採用増の反動や経験者中心の採用、
在宅勤務の広がりなども作用した可能性があると指摘している。

AI 曝露はAnthropic が作成した、AI の実際の利用状況を反映した作業単位の指標による。若手（職位の低い
層）向け・経験者向けの区分は Lightcast が求人の記載から判定したもの。求人の数の分析であり、実際に誰

そして先に紹介したスタンフォード大学の研究では 、在宅勤務や金利への感応度を調整して
も、22～25 歳の高曝露職と低曝露職の雇用の伸びの差は大きく変わらないが、学歴構成も含め
て調整するとその差は調整前の約 18％ポイントから約 8％ポイントに縮み、統計的に有意な関
係は確認できなくなる12。 
以上のように、一部の職種で若手の雇用が悪化していることは確認できるが、その原因を生
成 AI に求めるには慎重さが必要である。その影響を見極めるには、ChatGPT 公開前からの悪化
と、公開後に加わった可能性のある変化を分けて考える必要がある。また 、足元の弱さをコロ
ナ禍の採用増の反動だけで説明できるとは限らない。複数の要因が考えられる中で、本稿で取
り上げた研究でも、AI の影響の大きさについて明確な結論は得られていない。

3. 雇用者数だけでは捉えにくい仕事の機会の変化 
仕事を得る機会を考えるには、雇用者数が増えているかどうかに加え、どの分野で増えてい
るのか、新たな採用がどの程度行われているのかを見る必要がある。また、AI による省力化も、
人員数を変えずに業務や外注の見直しとして進む場合がある。以下では、これら三つの側面を
確認する。 
（1）雇用増は民間教育・医療に偏る 
米国では、ChatGPT が公開された2022 年 11 月から2026 年 8 月までに、情報や専門・事業サ
ービスなど、AI の影響を受けやすい職種の多い産業で雇用は減少した。一方、全体の雇用増を
支えたのは民間教育・医療である。同期間の雇用増約 480 万人のうち、約 3 分の 2（約 325 万
人）を占めた。ただし、直近 1 年の全体の雇用増は約 60 万人にとどまり、そのうち約 54 万人
を民間教育・医療が占める（図表6）。 
図表 6 業種別に見た雇用者数の増減（米国）

（注）その他は全体から掲載した業種を差し引いた残差。 
（出所）米労働統計局（BLS）より大和総研作成

12 この研究では、学歴構成は AI が仕事に影響する経路も反映している可能性があるため、その違いを調整し
て差が縮んでも、AI 以外の要因で説明できたとは限らない、としている。

民間教育・医療の雇用者数の伸びは、コロナ禍で落ち込んだ反動にもみえる。ただ、コロナ
禍前の2020 年2 月から見ても、雇用増約680 万人の半分（約340 万人）を占めており、雇用増
の最大の担い手であることは変わらない。全体の雇用増が、幅広い産業での雇用拡大を意味す
るわけではない。 
（2）低採用・低離職の下で就職・転職の機会は限られる 
雇用者数の動きと、新たに職を得る機会の違いは、採用と離職からも確認できる。 米国の求
人労働異動調査（JOLTS）によると、2026 年 1～7 月の採用率は平均 3.3％と、コロナ禍前の
2017～19 年（3.8％）を下回り、失業率が 7～8％台だった 2012～13 年並みに低い（図表 7）。
同じ期間の比較では、離職率も3.7％から3.3％に下がり、自己都合退職率（2.2％→2.0％）も
解雇率（1.2％→1.1％）も低下した。採用率の低さは若手だけの現象ではなく、労働市場全体
にみられる。 
図表 7 採用率と離職率（米国）

（出所）BLS より大和総研作成

離職が少なければ、欠員を埋める採用も生まれにくい。就職・転職の機会が限られる中、 先
行きへの不安から今の職にとどまる動きは「ジョブハギング（仕事にしがみつくこと） 」と呼
ばれ、2025 年 8 月ごろから米国の報道で広く取り上げられている13。米国の履歴書作成サイト
が2026 年2 月に行った調査では、自らをジョブハギングの状態にあると回答した就業者が57％
と、半年前の 45％から増えた。その 7 割は、今後半年以内に AI が自分の雇用に影響すること
を懸念していた 14。就職の入口に立つ人にとっては、いわば「空席の少ない市場」である。 
（3）AI による省力化の影響は、人員の削減より仕事の中身の見直しに表れている 
AI による省力化についても、雇用者数だけでは変化を捉えにくい。OECD が日本を含む7 カ国

13 例えば、Fortune “Workers are ‘job hugging’ in a stagnant labor market, but growing resentment 
means they could bail as soon as the next Great Resignation comes”（2025 年8 月 18 日）。 
14 ResumeBuilder.com “6 in 10 Workers Are Clinging To Their Jobs as Job Hugging Soars in 2026”
（2026 年2 月11 日）。米国の就業者2,188 人へのオンライン調査で、ジョブハギングを、会社への愛着では
なく不安から今の職にとどまることとしている。

### Page 9

1

9 / 13

の中小企業に行った調査では、生成 AI を使う企業の 83％が人員の必要数に影響はないと答え
た。必要な人員数の減少（9％）より、従業員の業務負担（33％）や外注への依存（14％）の
減少を挙げる企業が多い 15。社内の人員数が変わらなくても、外部に依頼する仕事は減りうる。 
前出のデンマークの研究では、時間を節約できた利用者の 85％が、その時間を他の業務に充
てると答え、雇用主が特段の施策を講じていない職場でも、利用者の8％が AI の出力の品質確
認など全く新しい業務を担っていた。研究者らは、こうした状況が、業務の見直しなど計測さ
れにくい投資が先行し、生産性の伸びが遅れて表れる「J カーブ」の初期段階と整合的だとし
ている 16。AI による省力化が、直ちに人員や労働時間の削減につながるのではなく、仕事の配
分を変える形で進んでいることがうかがえる。

4. 新たな仕事が生まれても、若手に回るとは限らない 
第 2 章で紹介した研究では、若手の雇用悪化は、主に「解雇」ではなく「採用されない」形
で表れている。この状態が続くかは、AI による省力化で生まれる余力を企業がどの業務に振り
向け、新たな需要がどれだけ人手を必要とするかにかかっている。 
省力化の後の展開は、主に三つの経路として整理できる 17。①人数を変えずに仕事の中身が
変わる。②既存の社員で対応できる範囲が広がり、採用・外注の抑制や人員削減につながる。
③値下げや品質向上による需要の拡大、新しい業務の創出が人手を必要とし、配置転換や採用
につながる。 
AI 導入後の雇用の動きには、企業規模による違いも見られる。デンマークの研究では、AI を
導入した小規模企業で新たな採用が抑えられ、雇用の増えるペースが鈍ったと報告されている。
一方、大規模企業では、同様の雇用の伸びの鈍化は確認されなかった18。 
②と③は、同じ企業の中で同時に進みうる。41 カ国の多国籍企業を対象とした研究は、AI導
入企業で増えた雇用がどの層に向かったかを示している。生成AI を導入した企業の拠点では、
ChatGPT 公開直後から若手（職位の低い層）の比率が下がり始め、2026 年 3 月には導入してい
ない企業の拠点での比率を 1.9％ポイント下回った（図表 8）19。この比率低下の主因は、若手

15 OECD [2025]. 対象はオーストリア、カナダ、ドイツ、アイルランド、日本、韓国、英国の従業員249 人以
下の企業 5,232 社（2024 年10～12 月）。割合は生成AI を利用する企業の回答で、一時点の自己申告による。 
16 Brynjolfsson, Rock, and Syverson [2021]. 生産性の計測に関する議論であり、雇用の推移を示したもの
ではない。 
17 Acemoglu and Restrepo [2019] の枠組みを基に整理した。 
18 Bonin, Darougheh, and Kuchler [2026]. 企業ごとにAI 導入前の雇用トレンドを調整し、2022 年にAI を利
用せず 2023 年に利用した企業と、両年とも非利用の企業を比較した。 
19 Chandar and Klein Teeselink [2026]. 導入企業は、2022 年 11 月以降に生成AI に関わる求人を1 件以上掲
載した企業。拠点全体の雇用も約 3.3％増えたが、5％水準では統計的に有意でない。研究者らは、導入企業の
伸びには導入していない企業からの売上の移転も含まれうるため、企業単位の効果は経済全体の雇用の変化と
一致するとは限らないとしている。日本も対象に含まれるが、データが日本の雇用を捉える割合は 2％程度に
とどまり、日本単独の推計は示されていない。Hosseini Maasoum and Lichtinger [2026] は、同じ Revelio
の職歴データを用いて米国の導入企業で若手の雇用減を示しており、対象や推計方法により結果は異なる。

### Page 10

1

10 / 13

の雇用減少ではなく、ChatGPT 公開から約 1 年半後に始まった経験者の雇用増加である。この
研究の著者らは、AI の影響を受けやすい職種では、AI が若手の仕事を省く一方で経験者への需
要を広げたと解釈している。需要が広がっても、その担い手が若手になるとは限らないことを
示す結果である。 
図表 8 生成 AI を導入した企業の海外拠点での雇用の変化（41 カ国）

（注）導入していない企業の拠点との差（2022 年10 月基準） 。点は推計値、帯は95％信頼区間。破線はChatGPT
公開（2022 年 11 月） 。若手は職歴データ（Revelio Labs）の職位区分 1～2、経験者は 3～7（年齢層の区分で
はない） 。雇用は対数差×100。親会社本社と同じ都市圏にある他の多国籍企業の導入率を操作変数とする推
計。表題・軸は和訳し、目盛りを％・％ポイントに換算した。 
（出所）Chandar and Klein Teeselink [2026] より大和総研作成（画像の加工・仮訳は大和総研）

③の経路を示す研究もある。米国の上場企業では、AI 投資を増やした企業ほど、主に製品の
革新を通じて売上と雇用が伸びたとされる 20。デンマークの研究で報告された、AI の出力の品
質確認などの新しい業務も③の芽といえる。 
採用の構成も、AI の使い方によって変わりうる。AI を使う既存社員が若手の入口の仕事を担
えば、採用は経験者に寄りうる。逆に、AI の支援で経験の浅い人に任せられる仕事が増えれば、

20 Babina et al. [2024]. 2010～2018 年の非テック上場企業の分析。効果は規模の大きい企業に集中する。他
社からの需要の移転も含みうるが、AI 投資の多い産業では産業全体でも売上と雇用が増えたとされる。

### Page 11

1

11 / 13

若手を採る利点は高まりうる。AI が経験の差を縮めるこうした作用は、「専門性の平準化」と
呼ばれる 21。 
もっとも、これまでの実証研究では、採用が経験者に寄る動きが目立つ。この 動きが続けば、
懸念されるのは「育成の空洞化」である。 経験者の多くは、入口の仕事を通じて育つ。若手採
用の絞り込みは、個々の企業には合理的でも、経済全体では将来の経験者層 を薄くしかねない。
AI の支援と経験者の指導を組み合わせ、若手が経験を積む場を残す工夫が求められる。 
日本企業もこの問題と無縁ではいられないだろう。日本では少子高齢化で人手不足が続き、
新卒を一括採用して社内で育てる企業が多い。このため、影響は採用数より、採る職種の構成
や、人手の足りない部署への配置転換に先に表れる可能性がある 22。とはいえ、採用数が保た
れても、AI が入口の仕事を担えば、若手が経験を積む場は細りうる。問われているのは「AI で
若手の仕事をどこまで省けるか」ではなく、 「AIを使いながら若手をどう育てるか」である。

以上

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -49.02 điểm tương ứng -2.66%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Y tế và Bất động sản là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -4,944 tỷ đồng, 
tập trung ở các mã VPB, TCB và VHM; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 1995 
tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -5,280 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua và đang 
duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế 3,630 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 208 hợp đồng nhưng đang 
duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,428 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ hai liên tiếp sau nâng hạng, khi VN-Index lùi 
về đóng cửa 1.737 điểm — giảm khoảng 2% so với tuần trước và đang kiểm định lại vùng 
đáy cũ của tháng 8, quanh 1.720. Áp lực bán duy trì khi chỉ số giảm nhiều phiên liên tiếp, 
cho thấy hiệu ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" sau nâng hạng vẫn chưa kết thúc, trong bối 
cảnh dòng tiền thận trọng và thanh khoản ở mức thấp. Khối ngoại tiếp tục là lực cản 
chính khi bán ròng mạnh cả 5 phiên trong tuần, riêng phiên 2/10 bán ròng tới 3.361 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở PNJ và HDB. Áp lực bên ngoài vẫn hiện diện khi Fed duy trì lập trường 
diều hâu và giá dầu ở mức cao; trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo KQKD sẽ bắt đầu có 
tác động, kỳ vọng theo chiều hướng tích cực, qua đó giúp nâng đỡ thị trường chung.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên khung tuần, xuyên thủng vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, cho thấy áp lực bán đã có phần quyết liệt hơn sau 2 tuần giằng co trước 
đó. Trên khung ngày, sau các phiên lình xình vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng dần chuyển sang 
diễn biến giảm điểm rõ nét hơn về cuối tuần, đồng thời cắt sâu xuống dưới SMA50, thể 
hiện áp lực phân phối đang có phần lấn át mặc dù chưa xảy ra hiện tượng bán tháo khi 
khối lượng khớp lệnh được giữ ở mức thấp. Diễn biến tính từ vùng đỉnh 1870 vào đầu tháng 
9 đang không mấy khả quan, tuy nhiên xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn, tính từ đáy 1640 vào cuối 
tháng 7, hiện vẫn đang được bảo lưu và VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ có nhịp hồi phục trở lại 
khi về đến vùng hỗ trợ kế tiếp tại quanh 1710. 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Acter, an established 
multinational cleanroom engineering, procurement, construction and 
management (EPCM) turnkey service provider, with a Buy (1) rating. Its 
core business includes cleanroom electromechanical engineering, 
water/gas/chemical delivery systems, and other cleanroom services. We 
believe now is a good time to revisit the stock as it underperformed the 
TAIEX Index by 32.7% in 3Q26, mainly on weaker-than-expected 2Q26 
results and soft sector sentiment. Given its stronger revenue momentum 
over recent months and upcoming TSMC results on 15 October, which may 
drive sentiment, we suggest investors to buy the stock. We forecast a 
24.6% revenue CAGR and 36.9% net profit CAGR over 2026-28E.

Intact global demand for cleanroom technology, the edge AI market, 
and rapid cleanroom expansion in Asia further support Acter’s order 
backlog. Its backlog reached TWD66bn in July 2026 vs. TWD46bn in 2025 
and TWD30bn in 2022 (15.3% 2022-25 CAGR), which largely outpaced the 
global cleanroom technology market’s forecast CAGR over 2023-2032E, 
according to Precedence Research.

Strong competitive edge supports order share gains across multiple 
segments. Acter’s competitive edge lies in 1) intelligent management 
enabled by digital twins and AI, using 3D building information modelling 
(BIM) and AI-driven digital twin management; 2) intellectualisation of green 
engineering, which comprises green design, green procurement, and green 
techniques; 3) experience across multiple industries (ie, data centres and 
electromechanical engineering for the consumer industry) which supports 
better integration; and 4) a complete network, enabling on-the-spot support. 
Acter believes it has the highest market share among the operators of three 
major US data centre players due largely to its capabilities in liquid cooling 
and its expertise in electromechanical and cleanroom engineering.

Catalysts: Near-term: project wins from key clients, supporting stronger 
monthly revenue momentum; mid- to long-term: an improving operating-
margin trend and a resilient order backlog.

Valuation: We are the first non-domestic broker to cover Acter. We initiate 
coverage with a 12-month TP of TWD1,415, based on a target PER of 20x 
(equivalent to a 0.54x PEG over 2026-28E). We believe our valuation is 
fair, given Acter’s strong earnings visibility, which we believe warrants a 
rerating. Our TP is supported by a DCF valuation of TWD1,454.

Risks: 1) weaker-than-expected expansion in the semiconductor industry; 
and 2) project delays or cancellations.

7 October 2026

Acter

Initiation: acting on the demand for cleanrooms

➢ Key beneficiary of the cleanroom shortage in the high-tech industry 
➢ Fast-growing order backlog likely to be realised over 2026-28E 
➢ Initiating with a Buy (1) rating and a 12-month TP of TWD1,415

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026

How do we justify our view? 
Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Acter: net profit and net-profit growth    
We forecast Acter to record a 36.9% net profit CAGR over 
2026-28E, on the back of a 24.6% revenue CAGR over the 
same period, driven by 1) larger project scale, as strong 
demand for cleanroom EPCM services from the high-tech 
sector is likely to support continued growth in Acter’s order 
backlog and 2) improving gross margins and operating 
efficiency. We expect operating margin to expand by 2.0pp 
in 2027E and 1.3pp in 2028E, driven by favourable gross 
margins on new projects and operating leverage.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Valuation   Acter: 1-year-forward PER bands    
Our 12-month TP of TWD1,415 is based on a target PER 
of 20x, applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Our 
target multiple of 20x is higher than the stock’s past-3-year 
1-year-forward PER (12.2x) and past-5-year 1-year-
forward PER (9.6x) and implies a PEG of 0.54x on our 
36.9% net profit CAGR forecast over 2026-28E. We 
believe the stock deserves a rerating in the next 12 
months, largely supported by resilient cleanroom demand 
globally.

Our TP is also supported by an alternative DCF-derived 
valuation of TWD1,454/share.

Source: Bloomberg, Daiwa forecasts

Earnings revisions

Acter: Bloomberg consensus EPS forecast revisions   
Bloomberg consensus EPS estimates for 2026E and 
2027E have risen 24.8% and 24.3% YTD, respectively. 
This is likely due to upward revisions to order backlog and 
earnings forecasts following better-than-expected industry 
outlook. We are the first foreign broker to cover Acter.

Source: Bloomberg

Company profile 
Established in 1979, Acter Group (Acter) is a leading engineering services provider specialising in 
cleanroom, electromechanical, and high-tech facility integration solutions for semiconductor, 
optoelectronics, biotech, green energy, and advanced manufacturing clients across Taiwan, China and 
the rest of the world. As of 2025, its revenue comprised cleanroom electromechanical (71%), water, gas 
and chemical delivery systems (20%), consumer industry electromechanical (4%), biomedical (2%) and 
high-tech equipment & materials sales & services (3%).

### Page 5

5

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Acting on the demand for cleanrooms 
Why are cleanrooms a bottleneck in the high-tech 
industry? 
In recent years, cleanrooms have become a critical bottleneck for high-tech industries, 
especially semiconductor manufacturing, OSAT, AI hardware, and PCB, mainly because of 
its indispensable nature, severe supply-demand imbalance, labour shortages, and high 
technical and regulatory barriers.

Cleanrooms are an indispensable core infrastructure as they determine product 
yield and quality stability. Advanced manufacturing nodes in semiconductors, 
optoelectronics, and biopharmaceuticals require highly particle-free, constant-temperature, 
and constant-humidity environments to ensure reliable production. As process 
technologies continue to advance, such as 2nm node (or even newer nodes) and 
advanced packaging, existing cleanrooms must be rebuilt, and the complexity of building 
new ones thus increases. This keeps overall demand for cleanrooms at high levels.

A severe global supply-demand imbalance with demand for cleanroom construction 
outpacing supply. The surge in AI servers, high-performance computing (HPC), and high-
bandwidth memory (HBM) has triggered a global expansion for front-end wafer fabs, 
packaging and testing facilities, and PCB manufacturing. Order visibility remains extremely 
strong across the industry. According to Markets and Markets, the global cleanroom market 
size is set to expand at a CAGR of 6.6% over 2025-31E. At the same time, the global 
semiconductor cleanroom market size is set to grow at a CAGR of 8.0% over 2025-30E. 
For instance, Acter’s order backlog exceeded TWD66bn at the end of July 2026, up from 
TWD46bn at the end of 2025, showing the urgency of demand for cleanroom construction.

Global: cleanroom market size and growth      Global: semiconductor cleanroom market size and growth

Source: Markets and Markets  Source: Markets and Markets

Human resources are an additional limitation to capacity growth. Cleanroom 
engineering is highly technical and labour-intensive, relying on specialised on-site 
installation and supervision that cannot be replaced by robots. At the same time, the 
industry faces severe labour shortages and an aging workforce, and younger generations 
are reluctant to join field operations. Cross-border staff deployment is also less cost-
efficient than local hiring.

Cleanrooms involve complex system integration, creating high barriers to entry. A 
cleanroom construction includes 4 sequential phases, where each phase usually requires 
totally different areas of expertise. Therefore, the competitive landscape in each area 
differs. The process begins with civil & structural (C&S) works to establish the physical 
plant, followed by mechanical, electrical, and plumbing (MEP) works for essential utility 
generation, where Acter is one of the leading global experts. After MEP is finished, 
chemicals/gas/water are then distributed via main systems through primary piping 
network and finally delivered to production equipment through hook-up systems. As

### Page 6

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
global supply chains restructure under N+1 (domestic market + 1) strategies, companies 
building overseas facilities in places like the US face strict and complicated licensing 
processes, particularly on fire safety and hazardous chemicals. Without experience dealing 
with such engineering and safety issues, construction timelines can be severely delayed.

As a result, even with advanced equipment and chip designs, a high-tech company cannot 
begin production without adequate cleanroom space, skilled engineers, and system-
integration capabilities. Therefore, cleanroom engineering contractors hold significant 
bargaining power and capacity allocation leverage in today's tech supply chain.

IC foundry 
In the foundry sector, where the cleanroom supply-demand shortage is the tightest, project 
activity remains centred on advanced process capacity expansion in Taiwan and Arizona. 
Simultaneously, domestic advanced node development remains intense, with multi-phase 
fab builds advancing across Hsinchu, Taichung, and Kaohsiung. Local cleanroom and MEP 
integration demand is further boosted by advanced packaging facilities in Taiwan (such as 
Chiayi and Tainan) and upcoming projects in the US are set to contribute revenue for the 
cleanroom providers in the following years.

Memory 
The memory segment is exhibiting robust momentum as well, mainly catalysed by HBM 
capacity additions. Projects such as the plant in Japan, transitioned to contribution for the 
cleanroom EPCM providers starting from 2026. In Taiwan, turnkey engineering contracts in 
Miaoli are also actively underway. This trend is reinforced by stronger-than-expected 
expansion activity from memory producers in China.

OSAT,  PCB and ODM/OEM 
For OSAT companies, demand is also surging due to the spill-over effect of advanced 
packaging protocols and N+1 supply chain restructuring. The OSAT names are expanding 
their footprint both in Southern Taiwan and in overseas locations including in the US and 
Southeast Asia. Major engineering contracts signed between OSAT clients and Acter show 
stable long-term workload for strong cleanroom demand over our forecast horizon. 
Similarly, overseas expansion for PCB manufacturing is peaking in Southeast Asia, 
especially in Thailand. We expect Chinese PCB players to also increase their capacity, just 
like memory companies in the past year. Except for front-end and back-end chip 
manufacturing, ODM/OEM and data centre infrastructure is also a key growth engine for 
the EPCM industry. Major Taiwanese ODMs are building production facilities in Texas and 
Mexico to meet local manufacturing needs. Cleanroom contractors, such as Acter, 
providing MEP and system integration in Texas are directly benefiting from these projects. 
The shift toward liquid cooling systems has also increased engineering complexity, 
favouring few cleanroom companies with the expertise to deliver complex integration for 
CSPs in both North America and Southeast Asia.

Acter: an integrated cleanroom solutions provider  
Acter Group (Acter), an integrated cleanroom solutions provider, maintains its competitive 
positioning within the high-tech cleanroom and MEP engineering sector, driven by its group 
synergy and global footprint. Acter operates as a comprehensive vendor through a group 
business model. The parent company, Acter, supervises overall cleanroom and MEP 
services, while subsidiary Nova Technology (Novatech, 6613 TT, not rated) mainly provides 
chemical primary system and hook-up, and Novatech’s subsidiary Rayzher (7703 TT, not 
rated) provides gas primary system, hook-up and equipment. In China, Acter operates 
through Acter Technology Integration Group (603163 CH, not rated) and Suzhou Winmax 
(not listed) for Chinese EPCM (engineering, procurement, construction, and management) 
and equipment business. Except for C&S (civil and structural) works, where the margin is 
low (7-8%) and the contract duration is long, Acter’s services span nearly the entire 
cleanroom construction process. Its one-stop solutions model supports customer retention 
Acter provides

solutions via various 
subsidiaries

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
and improves bid-win rates, particularly for overseas expansion, where customers have 
few reliable providers to choose from.

Acter: organisational chart

Source: Company, Daiwa

To avoid the risk of over-concentration, Acter diversifies its client portfolio across 
semiconductor foundries, memory, PCB, OSAT, ODM hardware assembly, CSP data 
centres and the consumer industry. By sector, semiconductor projects represent the largest 
share of Acter’s portfolio, accounting for 66% of total revenue in 1H26, vs. 69% in 2025. 
Foundry manufacturing is the largest source of demand for cleanrooms in Taiwan, 
especially as advanced-node capacity expands at sites in Hsinchu, Taichung and 
Kaohsiung. In the memory segment, the company executes turnkey engineering builds to 
expand its capacity aggressively, creating cleanroom opportunities across Taiwan, Japan 
and Singapore. This multi-sector coverage decreases the risk of cyclical downturns in any 
individual segment, in our view.

By establishing 33 operating entities across 11 international regions, the company 
effectively captures capex migration driven by global N+1 supply chain diversification. This 
allows Acter to support Taiwanese and international clients as they expand manufacturing 
capacity from Taiwan into Southeast Asia, Japan and the US. Due to the complexity of 
advanced-node IC manufacturing, packaging and testing, it is especially important for 
these Taiwanese semiconductor companies to work with familiar cleanroom builders when 
expanding in the US. This is one of the reasons why Taiwanese cleanroom companies are 
receiving overseas orders and starting to set up global bases, in our view. Despite higher 
management, material, and manpower costs in the US, most cleanroom companies are 
benefitting from the trend by improving margins, given the higher complexity and risks to 
operate overseas. Across overseas hubs in Texas and Phoenix (expected to begin from 
2027), Acter prioritises local hiring and partners with regional civil contractors to shorten 
the learning curve and to avoid the high overhead. To further address the potential labour 
shortages in the future, the company has established cross-border academic and 
vocational partnerships to bring Southeast Asian talent to Taiwan for technical training 
before deploying them back to their home markets, securing future human capacity to 
support global expansion projects.

By product, cleanroom electromechanical services remain Acter’s largest source of 
revenue (accounting for 66% of 1H26 revenue, per the company). These services include 
cleanroom architectural and MEP engineering, which powers airflow, filtration, 
environmental regulation and utility controls. Water, gas and chemical delivery systems are 
its second-largest source of revenue (accounting for 24% of 1H26 revenue, per the 
company). Contributed mainly by Novatech (6613 TT, not rated) and Rayzher (7703 TT, not 
rated), these services cover the main systems and hook-up systems, for  ultra-pure water 
(UPW), specialty gases, chemical piping, etc., all of which require highly precise build-up 
work. Except for cleanroom electromechanical and water, gas and chemical delivery 
systems, the rest of Acter’s revenue comes from the consumer industry services 
electromechanical (5%), biomedical (3%), and high-tech equipment and materials sales 
and services (1%).

Acter: revenue by product

Source: Company, Daiwa

Technically, Acter integrates digital transformation and green engineering into its business 
model, helping it differentiate from peers. Acter deploys digital twin technology and 1:1 3D 
BIM modelling to detect piping interferences and optimise spatial layout before installation, 
minimising schedule delays. This makes Acter a more reliable option, in our view, and thus 
being a favourable supplier, especially for the semiconductor clients that have extremely 
high quality and swiftness criterion.

Bright outlook: strong order visibility 
Rising orders on hand due to supply-demand imbalance for cleanrooms 
Acter's order backlog has increased from TWD55bn in 1Q26 to TWD60bn in 2Q26 and 
TWD66bn in July 2026. We expect this figure to further increase in the following quarters, 
given the supply-demand imbalance in the cleanroom sector. This is composed of 
semiconductor (63%), electronic assembly (11%), consumer (9%) and others (17%), per 
the company. In 2H25, the company accelerated the digestion of small-scale projects 
below TWD1bn, and, as a result, starting from 1H26, it successfully shifted its project

Back-end packaging, cloud infrastructure and hardware assembly are additional growth 
drivers. Acter serves as a contractor for both Taiwanese and overseas OSAT providers, 
including TWD21.7bn contracts YTD, awarded by a major OSAT client vs. TWD16.6bn 
contract obtained from the same client in 2025. In cloud infrastructure, Acter maintains 
qualified supplier status with US CSPs, providing mechanical, electrical and plumbing 
(MEP) integration, especially for those data centres that implement liquid-cooling systems. 
The company also wins factory construction projects for Taiwanese ODMs across the US 
and Southeast Asia, as well as PCB facility projects in Thailand and China. Non-tech 
sectors, including biotechnology, commercial buildings, smart logistics hubs and medical 
facilities, comprise 9% of the total backlog, per management.

Competitive advantages 
High entry barriers to the segment 
Comprehensive product portfolio, empowered by subsidiaries 
Acter provides a one-stop EPCM solution, leveraging its subsidiaries to deliver complete 
solutions. Within the group, the parent company Acter focuses on overall cleanroom and 
MEP system integration; subsidiary Novatech provides primary chemical systems and 
hook-up systems; and sub-subsidiary Rayzer specialises in primary specialty-gas systems 
and hook-up systems. In China, Suzhou Winmax, also a subsidiary of Novatech, focuses 
on the supply of wet processing and chemical handling equipment. This integrated model 
covers cleanroom engineering integration, water, power, chemical and gas-utility systems, 
as well as post-completion validation and maintenance, ensuring cross-system 
compatibility while mitigating risks associated with multi-contractor hiring.

Under global supply chain diversification trends, this unified group strategy strengthens 
order-winning capabilities in overseas markets. Clients expanding internationally face 
regulatory, linguistic and supply-chain complexities, favouring partners that offer one-stop 
services with a proven cross-border track record.

A diverse client base helps Acter weather economic cycles 
Acter’s main competitive advantage is its diversification of clients by industry and region. 
This guides the company through the peaks and troughs of the economy, which is 
especially crucial given the strong cyclicality of the semiconductor/high-tech industry.  Acter 
serves a broad client base across semiconductors, OSAT, PCB, electronics assembly 
(ODM), CSP data centres, biopharmaceuticals and even the consumer industry (including 
commercial real estate and logistics), whereas many peers rely on a few large foundry 
clients. When semiconductor demand slows, capacity expansion in the PCB or data centre 
sectors offset the decline. Additionally, Acter avoids concentration risk and margin 
compression from major foundry accounts, as projects from leading foundries yield lower 
margins; by serving niche markets like US-based CSPs, the company achieves higher 
gross margins.

Geographically, Acter operates across Taiwan, China, Southeast Asia, the US and other 
countries. The company has OSAT and foundry customers in Taiwan and China, PCB 
clients expanding into Thailand, and ODM and data centre clients establishing operations 
in the US. Across different countries, local hiring and local supply chain implementation 
can further reduce costs and increase efficiency relative to peers reliant on a cross-border 
labour force.

Labour shortages 
Amid global labour shortages, Acter minimises the operational impact and turns workforce 
constraints into a moat through localised talent development. Since engineering labour 
cannot easily be automated, Acter has built a cross-border talent pipeline via industry-
academia partnerships. The company recruits Southeast Asian students for specialised 
M&E engineering training in Taiwan, deploying them to global project sites after their 
graduation to solve labour bottlenecks. This structure maintains an engineering team with 
an average tenure of more than 7 years, creating an operational barrier in an industry with 
a high employee turnover rate.

Compared with peers relying on high-cost personnel sent from Taiwan, we note that Acter’s 
local hiring strategy creates cost and efficiency advantages. Prioritising compliant local 
hiring across overseas markets reduces travel expenses while accelerating integration with 
local supply chains and regulations. Localised teams also handle post-completion services 
and technical support directly on-site, building long-term client trust in overseas markets.

The supply of cleanroom 
services is limited due to 
high entry barriers in the 
segment

### Page 11

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Numerous competitors, each with different specialisation 
Acter faces different competitors across its main product lines. In the EPCM segment, 
including cleanroom and MEP works, key competitors include UIS (2404 TT, not rated), 
L&K (6139 TT, not rated), Yankey (6691 TT, not rated) and Jiuhan (6903 TT, not rated). 
Despite having business models similar to Acter, UIS, Yankey and Jiuhan focus more on 
major semiconductor players in both Taiwan and the US, and L&K concentrates on ASEAN 
semiconductor projects. In the equipment/material sales and agency space, Acter mainly 
competes with Marketech (6196 TT, not rated). In the water, gas and chemical delivery 
systems area, through Novatech and Rayzher, Acter group competes with Marketech, 
Mega Union (6944 TT, not rated), Taiwan Puritic (6826 TT, not rated), Trusval (6667 TT, not 
rated) and Cica-Huntek (6725 TT, not rated).

In our view, despite the competitive environment, Acter maintains a formidable competitive 
moat built on several key advantages. We attribute this to its more than 45 year experience 
in the industry, enabling it to develop specialised expertise that is hard for competitors to 
replicate.

Financial analysis 
We forecast the company’s net profit to rise at a CAGR of 36.9% over 2026-28E, driven by 
a revenue CAGR of 24.6% due to surging demand from the high-tech industry (an increase 
in the order backlog to TWD66bn at the end of July 2026 vs. TWD46bn at end-2025) and a 
3.3pp expansion in operating margin over 2026-28E, driven by the gross margin 
improvement from more favourable project wins and operating leverage.

Strong order visibility, with revenue expected to grow 
across all sectors over 2026-28E 
We forecast Acter’s revenue to increase at a CAGR of 24.6% over 2026-28E, driven by 
scale expansion and a growing demand from various industries, including semiconductor, 
electronic assembly, optoelectronics and others.

Acter: revenue growth trend

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Gross- and operating-margin expansion trend  
Over 2026-28E, we forecast Acter’s gross and operating margins to expand on rising 
revenue scale and a higher complexity of cleanrooms for advanced manufacturing. We 
note that the gross margin from high-tech cleanrooms is higher than that from consumer 
industries, such as commercial buildings and biopharmaceuticals. Thus, we estimate the 
company’s gross margin to reach 26.0% in 2028E from 18.9% in 2025 and its operating 
margin to reach 21.6% in 2028E from 13.7% in 2025.

Acter: gross- and operating-margin trend

14.2% in 2028E (vs. 11.7% in 2026E) and for its net profit to grow at a CAGR of 36.9%

Intact demand to support revenue growth over our

We forecast Acter’s total cash and equivalents to reach TWD16,838m (36.8% of its total 
assets) in 2026E and increase to TWD27,839m in 2028E (42.8% of total assets) with net 
cash likely to come in at TWD16,244m in 2026E and TWD27,245m in 2028E.

After factoring in our 2027 and 2028 capex assumptions of TWD180m, we expect Acter’s 
free cash flow (FCF) to improve to TWD12,129m in 2028E, from TWD5,763m in 2026E. 
We believe the capacity expansion cost will initially be low and that the company will 
continue to benefit from the better profitability.

We expect Acter’s ROE to improve to a record-high of 56.8% in 2028E from 29.3% in 2025 
(following steady annual increases to 46.8% in 2026E and 54.9% in 2027E), which we 
attribute to margin expansion from the revenue size increase and gross margin expansion 
led by increases in cleanroom complexity.

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Valuation and risks  
Undemanding valuation, alongside its leading global 
positions in the OSAT and memory segments and an 
optimistic industry outlook  
We initiate coverage of Acter with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD1,415, based 
on a target PER of 20x applied to our one-year-forward EPS forecast. This target multiple 
is higher than the average of its past-3-year 1-year-forward PER of 12.2x (ranged from 
7.4x to 23.5x) and the average of its past-5-year 1-year-forward PER of 9.6x (ranged from 
4.4x to 23.5x), which we view as undemanding as we forecast a 36.9% net profit CAGR 
over 2026-28E, implying a 0.54x PEG. This is supported by its business expansion in the 
next 2-3 years. We see potential for a rerating of the stock, given Acter’s global leadership 
in cleanroom EPCM, particularly in the OSAT and memory industries.

The stock is trading currently at a 15.8x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 
36.9% over 2026-28E, translating to a 0.43x PEG ratio. Its share price has underperformed 
TAIEX Index by 32.7% in 3Q26, mainly due to its weaker-than-expected 2Q26 results. 
However, given the potential improvement in sector sentiment following TSMC’s results on 
15 October 2026, along with the likelihood that Acter’s revenue momentum will strengthen 
in the coming months, we recommend that investors buy the stock.

Acter: past-3-year-forward PER (average: 12.0x)    Acter: past-5-year-forward PER (average: 9.5x)

Source: Bloomberg, Daiwa estimates  Source: Bloomberg, Daiwa estimates

We compare Acter with other leading cleanroom EPCM companies such as UIS (2404 TT, 
TWD1,330.0, not rated), L&K (6139 TT, TWD787.0, not rated) and Yankey (6691 TT, 
TWD776.0, not rated). UIS, L&K and Yankey are trading at a PER of 14.7x (earnings 
CAGR of 40.7% over 2025-27E), 9.5x (earnings CAGR of 36.9% over 2026-28E) and 
15.1x (earnings CAGR of 53.6% over 2025-27E), on their 1-year-forward EPS, 
respectively.

Acter’s peers are trading at 18.7x for 2026E and 14.2x for 2027E. We forecast Acter to 
deliver an EPS CAGR of 36.9% over 2026-28E vs. 31.5% CAGR for the segment over the 
same period.

We believe our target one-year-forward PER of 20x (equivalent to a 0.54x PEG using 
2026-28E EPS CAGR) is undemanding as: 1) Acter is ramping up capacity to meet 
growing demand from high-tech customers and is in an expansion cycle; and 2) its 
leading position among OSAT and memory clients warrants a valuation premium, 
compared to its global peers.

Risks to our call  
Primary/key risks: 
Weaker-than-expected expansion in semiconductor industry 
The biggest downside risk to our call for Acter is weaker-than-expected expansion from 
semiconductor clients, especially in advanced-node foundries, advanced packaging and 
OSAT. If global demand for AI servers, HPC or HBM slows, or if clients delay fab build-ups 
and overseas expansion plans, it will directly pressure order inflows and backlog 
conversion for Acter.

Project execution delays or cancellations 
Acter’s revenue recognition is tied to large, complex EPCM and integrated engineering 
projects, which are often subject to customer-driven delays. Any postponement in project 
Our 12-month TP of 
TWD1,415 is also 
supported by our DCF 
valuation

### Page 18

18

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
schedule, construction bottlenecks or cancellation of large projects could defer revenue 
recognition and hurt overall profit. This risk is more obvious in overseas projects, where 
licensing, fire-safety approvals and hazardous-chemical regulations are more complicated.

Higher-than-expected raw-material price hikes 
Raw-material costs (especially copper and oil-related costs) account for 50-60% of COGS 
and thus the price volatility makes costs difficult to manage. Acter needs to maintain 
supplier relations, negotiate appropriate price-adjustment mechanisms with clients, and 
invest in R&D to identify composite material substitutes, thereby reducing reliance on any 
single component.

Secondary risks:  
Labour issues or larger-than-expected increases in labour costs  
Cleanroom engineering is highly labour-intensive and relies on on-site installation and 
supervision. Although Acter has built a local hiring and talent-development model, a larger-
than-expected shortage of engineers or rising wage pressure could still raise costs and 
constrain capacity expansion. Any inability to secure enough skilled labour may also limit 
the company’s ability to convert orders into revenue in time.

Margin pressure from lower-margin projects or mix shift 
Acter’s business mix includes a few low-margin C&S services as well as larger EPCM and 
system integration contracts. If the company takes on a higher proportion of lower-margin 
projects, or if competition intensifies in certain geographies or customer segments, gross 
margin could come under pressure. In addition, an unfavourable product mix shift towards 
less profitable work could offset benefits from backlog growth.

Overseas expansion and regulatory risks 
Acter is expanding across markets in the US, Southeast Asia, Japan and China, which 
exposes it to FX movements, local compliance requirements, tax complexity and regulatory 
uncertainty. Delays in licensing, changes in safety standards or local-content requirements 
could increase project costs and reduce execution efficiency. Any misstep in managing 
overseas operations could damage profitability and client retention. 
Key risks to our call: 
weaker-than-expected 
expansion in 
semiconductor industry, 
project delays or 
cancellations, and 
higher-than-expected 
raw-material price hikes

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Appendix 
Company background 
History 
Acter (5536 TT), founded in 1979 and headquartered in Taichung, Taiwan, is a cleanroom 
EPCM provider with over four decades of experience. Starting with early mechanical and 
electrical (M&E) engineering through vertical and horizontal integration, Acter has now 
developed into an engineering technology group with cross-border execution capabilities. A 
cleanroom building process begins with civil & structural (C&S) works to establish the 
physical plant, followed by mechanical, electrical, and plumbing (MEP) works for 
essential utility generation, where Acter is one of the experts. After MEP is finished, 
chemicals/gas/water are then distributed via main systems through primary piping 
networks, and finally delivered to production equipment through hook-up systems. The 
company’s core competency is providing one-stop integrated total solutions, spanning 
services, primary piping networks and hook-up systems, leaving C&S services to other 
engineering peers, given the nature of the latter segment’s low margin and long timespan.

In terms of business model, Acter delivers EPCM (Engineering, Procurement, 
Construction, Management) capabilities. From initial customised planning and feasibility 
assessments to strategic procurement, installation, and post-completion maintenance, 
Acter builds production environments tailored to client process requirements.

Acter: EPCM solutions offered

Source: Company, Daiwa

Regarding global expansion, Acter actively responds to the global supply chain’s “N+1” 
strategy, operating across 11 regions with 33 companies globally. Its service network spans 
Taiwan, China, Southeast Asia (including Thailand, Malaysia, Vietnam, Indonesia, and 
Singapore), Japan, India, and the US. The company leverages a group synergy strategy 
by integrating resources from key subsidiaries – such as Novatech (6613 TT, not rated; 
provides chemical primary and hook-up systems), Rayzher (7703 TT, not rated; provides 
special gas primary and hook-up supply systems), and Acter Group (603163 CH, not rated; 
provides cleanroom engineering in China) – to form a complete technical ecosystem, 
ensuring cross-border capability and quality.

cleanroom EPCM 
provider in Taiwan; 
dominates both high-
tech market and

### Page 20

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Acter: global footprint

Source: Company, Daiwa

In 2Q26, Acter classified revenue as: cleanroom electromechanical (63%), water, gas and 
chemical delivery systems (28%), consumer industry electromechanical (6%), biomedical 
(2%) and high-tech equipment & materials sales & services (1%). In 2025, the product mix 
was: cleanroom electromechanical (71%), water, gas and chemical delivery systems 
(20%), consumer industry electromechanical (4%), biomedical (2%) and high-tech 
equipment & materials sales & services (3%).

Acter serves a diversified client base divided into high-tech and consumer divisions. The 
high-tech division focuses on advanced sectors including semiconductors, OSAT, PCB, AI 
data centres (for CSPs), and electronics assembly. The consumer division extends into 
healthcare, premium commercial properties, logistics facilities, and green engineering. This 
multi-industry strategy allows the company to capitalise on tech sector upgrades while 
maintaining resilient operations across technology (especially semiconductor) cycles.

Acter adopts a management strategy involving local hiring in overseas markets, prioritising 
collaboration with local supply chains and compliance with local regulations to reduce 
cross-border deployment costs and thus build strong local teams. Acter has more than 
2,600 employees worldwide (average age: 42 years; average length of service: 7 years). 
With more than 45 years of experience in EPCM, the company plays a reliable role for both 
high-tech and consumer industry.

saving construction methods, digital twins, ice-storage energy conservation, wastewater 
recycling, and AI smart facility management systems to help clients achieve carbon 
neutrality while expanding capacity. Backed by solid corporate governance, Acter

Glossary of terms 
EPCM (engineering, procurement, construction, and management): Cleanroom 
construction services that cover the whole process including project design, purchasing, 
construction and management.

BIM (building information modelling): A digital modelling system of a building and its key 
information used in cleanroom construction.

C&S (civil & structural): Site infrastructure and the design and construction of load-
bearing elements.

MEP (mechanical, electrical, and plumbing): Building essential systems for environment 
control, power, lighting, water, and drainage.

### Page 22

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 Oct 2026 
Source:  Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Acter maintains a structured governance framework that supports disciplined management. The
chairman is Chin-Li Liang. The company operates with defined responsibility across the board,
management, cyber security oversight, operations, and audit functions. It has established an
organisation function led by the President, a Chief Cyber Security Officer, a Response Team, an
Operations and Policy Enforcement Team, and an Audit Team to supervise policy execution and
review security implementation.
Capital management 1
Acter manages capital in a stable and shareholder-friendly manner. Over the past several years,
Acter has typically maintained a dividend payout ratio of 65-80%. It also maintains a strong
financing network across multiple banks, supporting flexibility for project execution and
expansion while preserving financial stability.
Related party &
transaction 1
Acter’s business is supported by a transparent network of suppliers, project partners, and
financial counterparties, including companies with equity ties and reciprocal business
relationships that are common in its operating ecosystem. These relationships are managed
through formal internal procedures for fund lending, endorsements and guarantees, and asset
acquisition and disposal, all of which are approved through shareholder mechanisms and
company rules. Such a structure helps keep inter-company and supply-chain transactions
organised and traceable, reducing the risk of unclear dealings.
E Product quality &safety 1
Acter executes its projects through a complete, disciplined process covering bidding
assessment, project planning, design, procurement, construction management, verification and
testing, completion and acceptance, closing review, and warranty service. Its cleanroom and
electromechanical engineering business is built around maintaining temperature, humidity, and
dust-free environments that support high-precision manufacturing. The company strengthens
quality control through BIM, Revit, simulation software, ISO drawing output tools, and automated
modeling, which improve design accuracy, construction efficiency, and project consistency.
E Ecological impacts 1
Acter has built a concrete sustainability programme with measurable environmental outcomes.
The company completed Scope 1, 2, and 3 greenhouse gas emissions calculation, obtained
third-party verification under ISO 14064-1:2018, and publicly disclosed energy consumption and
emissions data. It also implemented a strong waste reduction programme, achieving a 95%
wastepaper recycling rate at headquarters in 2025 and 100% paper recycling at the 19F and 20F
floors after establishing paper segregation and reuse procedures. The company also promoted
sustainability measures through emissions inventory management, environmental disclosures,
and resource-efficiency practices. These actions show active environmental management and a
clear commitment to lowering operational impact.
Health & safety
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

EQUITY: TECHNOLOGY
Genius Electronic Optical   3406.TW 3406 TT

EBO and CPO an ideal leverage of its capabilities 
Quantifying EBO and CPO opportunities; reiterate Buy
Action: Maintain Buy; raise TP to TWD1,415, implying ~34% upside
We expect AI optical interconnect and communication opportunities (e.g. co-packaged optics 
fiber array unit [CPO FAU], expanded beam optical [EBO], micro multi-fiber connector [MMC]) 
to transform the traditional optical lens industry over the next five years, and believe 
Taiwanese handset camera lens makers (e.g. Largan [3008 TT, Buy] and Genius) possess 
inherent advantages to cater to these new markets, given their high-precision molding+plastic 
injection, optical design, active alignment, optical coating and optical inspection capabilities, as 
well as clean-room facilities and sufficient free cash flow from cash-cow handset lens 
businesses.
In addition to holding the position of Apple’s (AAPL US, Not rated) No. 2 lens supplier, we believe 
Genius has obtained 3M’s (MMM US, Not rated) EBO ferrule order and will start shipping by end-
2026F or early-2027F. Genius appears well-positioned to supply various CPO FAU 
components (lens, prism, V groove, FA, etc.) and may potentially handle FAU assembly if 
requested by customers. We estimate the EBO and CPO-related new businesses will 
contribute <3% and 16-20% earnings for Genius in 2027F and 2028F, respectively (details 
in Fig. 10). Yet, given the high dollar content and long-term growth potential of CPO/FAU related 
products over the next five years, we believe optical lens makers with inherent advantages in this 
new field deserve stock re-rating; we thus raise our target P/E to 25x (near the high end of the 
historical range of 10-30x since 2020), from 17x. We reiterate Buy, and raise our TP to 
TWD1,415, based on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD56.61 (vs. prior TP of TWD568, based on 17x 
2025F EPS of TWD33.4, set two years ago). Genius trades at 25x 2027F P/E.
Apple: Sunny’s market share loss and continued camera upgrades in 2027-28F
Sunny (2382 HK, Neutral) earlier faced some yield issues with the new variable aperture (VA) 
main camera lens for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and this could benefit Largan (more directly) and 
Genius (indirectly) in the competitive landscape. It remains uncertain whether Genius will be 
introduced as a third lens supplier to iPhone Pro’ main camera in the future, and this poses an 
upside risk to our forecasts. However, given the headwinds of rising memory costs and the 
potential cannibalization of the 18 Pro series by the iPhone Duo, we forecast slower iPhone lens 
shipments from end-2026F to 1H27F, before the 20th anniversary iPhone (Pro Max) and the 
second-gen iPhone Duo are introduced in 2H27F. We expect further upgrades to the iPhone Pro 
main camera in 2H27F, and introduction of a new periscope camera for the Pro Max in 2H28F.

Company profile
Genius is the second source in Apple's lenses, and is gaining share in recent years. VR is a new growth engine for the company, with Facebook and
Apple being its key VR customers.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD1,415 is based on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD56.6. Our target P/E of 25x is near the high end of Genius' historical range of 10-30x since
2020. The benchmark index for this stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price

Downside risks are: 1) worse-than-expected iPhone demand, 2) rising ASP pressure due to competition, 3) worse-than-expected progress in
market-share gain in iPhone rear cam lenses, and 4) slower-than-expected penetration into other Android customers.
ESG
The company does not disclose any ESG information. However, based on industry features, we believe Genius is not a labor-intensive corporation
and it should not cause severe environmental issues.
